### 📄 scripts\migrate_webpush.py  ⭐
> SELA 樂透一路發 - Web Push 資料庫遷移
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

```py
"""
SELA 樂透一路發 - Web Push 資料庫遷移
執行方式: python scripts/migrate_webpush.py
"""
import os
import sys

# 添加專案根目錄到 path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from sqlalchemy import text
from app.core.database import engine


def migrate():
    """建立 Web Push 相關資料表和欄位"""
    
    migrations = [
        # 建立 push_subscriptions 表
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS push_subscriptions (
            id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
            user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            endpoint TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            p256dh_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
            auth_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
            user_agent VARCHAR(500),
            device_name VARCHAR(100),
            is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE NOT NULL,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
            last_used_at TIMESTAMP
        );
        """,
        
        # 建立索引
        """
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_push_subscriptions_user_id 
        ON push_subscriptions(user_id);
        """,
        
        """
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_push_subscriptions_active 
        ON push_subscriptions(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
        """,
        
        # users 表新增通知設定欄位
        """
        ALTER TABLE users 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS notify_draw_reminder BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE;
        """,
        
        """
        ALTER TABLE users 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS notify_win_alert BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE;
        """,
        
        """
        ALTER TABLE users 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS notify_settlement BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE;
        """,
    ]
    
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        for sql in migrations:
            try:
                conn.execute(text(sql))
                conn.commit()
                print(f"✅ 執行成功: {sql.strip()[:60]}...")
            except Exception as e:
                if "already exists" in str(e).lower() or "duplicate" in str(e).lower():
                    print(f"⏭️ 已存在，跳過")
                else:
                    print(f"❌ 執行失敗: {e}")
    
    print("\n🎉 Web Push 遷移完成！")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    migrate()
```

### 📄 scripts\seed_data.py  ⭐
> SELA 樂透一路發 - 種子資料腳本
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

```py
"""
SELA 樂透一路發 - 種子資料腳本
"""
import sys
import os

# 加入專案根目錄到 path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from app.core.database import SessionLocal, engine, Base
from app.models import LotteryType
from app.models.lottery_type import DEFAULT_LOTTERY_TYPES


def seed_lottery_types(db):
    """初始化彩種資料"""
    print("📝 初始化彩種資料...")
    
    for type_data in DEFAULT_LOTTERY_TYPES:
        # 檢查是否已存在
        existing = db.query(LotteryType).filter(
            LotteryType.code == type_data["code"]
        ).first()
        
        if existing:
            print(f"  ⏭️  {type_data['name']} 已存在,跳過")
            continue
        
        lottery_type = LotteryType(
            code=type_data["code"],
            name=type_data["name"],
            description=type_data["description"],
            price_per_bet=type_data["price_per_bet"],
            number_rules=type_data["number_rules"],
            prize_structure=type_data["prize_structure"],
            draw_days=type_data["draw_days"],
            draw_time=type_data["draw_time"],
            sort_order=type_data["sort_order"],
            is_active=True
        )
        db.add(lottery_type)
        print(f"  ✅ {type_data['name']} 已建立")
    
    db.commit()
    print("✨ 彩種資料初始化完成!")


def main():
    """執行種子資料初始化"""
    print("🌱 開始初始化種子資料...")
    print("=" * 50)
    
    # 建立所有表
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        seed_lottery_types(db)
    finally:
        db.close()
    
    print("=" * 50)
    print("🎉 種子資料初始化完成!")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
### 📄 scripts\set_admin.py  ⭐
> SELA 樂透一路發 - 設定管理員腳本
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SELA 樂透一路發 - 設定管理員腳本

用法:
    python scripts/set_admin.py              # 將第一個用戶設為管理員
    python scripts/set_admin.py --user-id=1  # 將指定用戶設為管理員
    python scripts/set_admin.py --list       # 列出所有用戶
"""
import sys
import os
import argparse

# 確保可以 import app 模組
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from app.core.database import SessionLocal, check_database_connection
from app.models.user import User, UserRole


def list_users(db):
    """列出所有用戶"""
    users = db.query(User).order_by(User.id).all()
    
    if not users:
        print("📭 目前沒有任何用戶")
        return
    
    print("\n📋 用戶列表:")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"{'ID':<6} {'角色':<10} {'名稱':<20} {'LINE ID':<20}")
    print("-" * 60)
    
    for user in users:
        role_display = "👑 管理員" if user.role == UserRole.ADMIN else "👤 一般"
        print(f"{user.id:<6} {role_display:<10} {user.display_name:<20} {user.line_user_id[:15]+'...':<20}")
    
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"共 {len(users)} 位用戶\n")


def set_admin(db, user_id: int = None):
    """設定管理員"""
    if user_id:
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
        if not user:
            print(f"❌ 找不到 ID 為 {user_id} 的用戶")
            return False
    else:
        # 取第一個用戶
        user = db.query(User).order_by(User.id).first()
        if not user:
            print("❌ 目前沒有任何用戶，請先登入系統")
            return False
    
    if user.role == UserRole.ADMIN:
        print(f"ℹ️  用戶 {user.display_name} (ID: {user.id}) 已經是管理員")
        return True
    
    # 設定為管理員
    user.role = UserRole.ADMIN
    db.commit()
    
    print(f"✅ 成功將 {user.display_name} (ID: {user.id}) 設為系統管理員")
    return True


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="SELA 樂透一路發 - 管理員設定工具")
    parser.add_argument("--user-id", type=int, help="指定用戶 ID")
    parser.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="列出所有用戶")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    print("🔧 SELA 管理員設定工具")
    print("=" * 40)
    
    # 檢查資料庫連線
    if not check_database_connection():
        print("❌ 無法連線到資料庫")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    print("✅ 資料庫連線成功")
    
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        if args.list:
            list_users(db)
        else:
            set_admin(db, args.user_id)
            print("\n📋 目前用戶狀態:")
            list_users(db)
    finally:
        db.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
### 📄 scripts\匯整子程式.py  ⭐
> 專案整合工具 - 把資料夾結構和程式碼整合成單一檔案
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
專案整合工具 - 把資料夾結構和程式碼整合成單一檔案
用途：上傳到 Claude 專案作為 context
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ===== 設定 =====
IGNORE_DIRS = {'.git', '__pycache__', '.venv', 'venv', 'node_modules', '.idea', '.vscode', 'dist', 'build', '__MACOSX'}
IGNORE_FILES = {'.DS_Store', 'Thumbs.db', '.gitignore', '*.pyc', '*.pyo'}
CODE_EXTENSIONS = {'.py', '.js', '.ts', '.html', '.css', '.json', '.yaml', '.yml', '.md', '.txt', '.sql', '.sh', '.env.example'}
MAX_FILE_SIZE = 100 * 1024  # 100KB，超過的檔案只顯示路徑不顯示內容


def should_ignore(path: Path) -> bool:
    """判斷是否要忽略此路徑"""
    if path.name in IGNORE_DIRS or path.name in IGNORE_FILES:
        return True
    if path.name.startswith('.') and path.name not in {'.env.example'}:
        return True
    return False


def generate_tree(root: Path, prefix: str = "") -> list[str]:
    """產生目錄樹狀圖"""
    lines = []
    items = sorted(root.iterdir(), key=lambda x: (x.is_file(), x.name.lower()))
    items = [x for x in items if not should_ignore(x)]
    
    for i, item in enumerate(items):
        is_last = i == len(items) - 1
        connector = "└── " if is_last else "├── "
        
        if item.is_dir():
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{connector}{item.name}/")
            extension = "    " if is_last else "│   "
            lines.extend(generate_tree(item, prefix + extension))
        else:
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{connector}{item.name}")
    
    return lines


def collect_files(root: Path) -> list[Path]:
    """收集所有程式碼檔案"""
    files = []
    for path in root.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file() and not should_ignore(path):
            # 檢查父目錄是否在忽略清單
            if any(p.name in IGNORE_DIRS for p in path.parents):
                continue
            if path.suffix.lower() in CODE_EXTENSIONS or path.name in {'Dockerfile', 'Makefile', 'requirements.txt', 'Procfile'}:
                files.append(path)
    return sorted(files, key=lambda x: str(x).lower())


def bundle_project(target_dir: str, output_file: str = None):
    """主程式：整合專案"""
    root = Path(target_dir).resolve()
    
    if not root.exists():
        print(f"❌ 找不到資料夾: {root}")
        return
    
    if output_file is None:
        output_file = f"{root.name}_bundle.txt"
    
    output_path = Path(output_file).resolve()
    
    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out:
        # 標題
        out.write(f"{'='*70}\n")
        out.write(f"專案名稱: {root.name}\n")
        out.write(f"整合時間: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\n")
        out.write(f"{'='*70}\n\n")
        
        # 目錄結構
        out.write("## 目錄結構\n\n")
        out.write("```\n")
        out.write(f"{root.name}/\n")
        for line in generate_tree(root):
            out.write(f"{line}\n")
        out.write("```\n\n")
        
        # 檔案內容
        out.write(f"{'='*70}\n")
        out.write("## 檔案內容\n")
        out.write(f"{'='*70}\n\n")
        
        files = collect_files(root)
        
        for filepath in files:
            rel_path = filepath.relative_to(root)
            out.write(f"\n{'─'*70}\n")
            out.write(f"### {rel_path}\n")
            out.write(f"{'─'*70}\n\n")
            
            # 檢查檔案大小
            if filepath.stat().st_size > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
                out.write(f"（檔案過大，略過內容：{filepath.stat().st_size / 1024:.1f} KB）\n")
                continue
            
            try:
                content = filepath.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                # 根據副檔名加上 code block
                lang = filepath.suffix.lstrip('.') or 'text'
                if lang == 'txt':
                    lang = 'text'
                out.write(f"```{lang}\n")
                out.write(content)
                if not content.endswith('\n'):
                    out.write('\n')
                out.write("```\n")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                out.write("（二進位檔案，略過）\n")
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"（讀取錯誤：{e}）\n")
        
        # 統計
        out.write(f"\n{'='*70}\n")
        out.write(f"## 統計\n")
        out.write(f"共 {len(files)} 個檔案\n")
        out.write(f"{'='*70}\n")
    
    print(f"✅ 完成！輸出檔案: {output_path}")
    print(f"   共整合 {len(files)} 個檔案")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        # 預設使用目前目錄
        target = "."
    else:
        target = sys.argv[1]
    
    output = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None
    bundle_project(target, output)
```

### 📄 static\admin.html  ⭐
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-TW">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>管理後台 - SELA 樂透一路發</title>

    <!-- Toast -->
    <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type} show`;
            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.classList.remove('show');
            }, 3000);
        }
        
        async function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = '/';
                return null;
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/users/me`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Unauthorized');
                
                const user = await response.json();
                if (user.role !== 'admin') {
                    showToast('需要管理員權限', 'error');
                    setTimeout(() => window.location.href = '/dashboard', 1500);
                    return null;
                }
                
                return token;
            } catch (error) {
                localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
                window.location.href = '/';
                return null;
            }
        }
        
        async function loadDashboard() {
            const token = await checkAuth();
            if (!token) return;
            
            // 載入統計
            try {
                const statsRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/dashboard`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (statsRes.ok) {
                    const stats = await statsRes.json();
                    document.getElementById('total-users').textContent = stats.total_users || 0;
                    document.getElementById('total-series').textContent = stats.total_series || 0;
                    document.getElementById('total-invested').textContent = formatMoney(stats.total_invested || 0);
                    document.getElementById('total-prize').textContent = formatMoney(stats.total_prize || 0);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('載入統計失敗', e);
            }
            
            // 載入用戶列表
            try {
                const usersRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/users?limit=10`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (usersRes.ok) {
                    const users = await usersRes.json();
                    renderUserList(users);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                document.getElementById('user-list').innerHTML = '<div class="loading">載入失敗</div>';
            }
            
            // 載入系列團列表
            try {
                const seriesRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/series?limit=10`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (seriesRes.ok) {
                    const series = await seriesRes.json();
                    renderSeriesList(series);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                document.getElementById('series-list').innerHTML = '<div class="loading">載入失敗</div>';
            }
            
            // 🔄 自動同步開獎資料（每天一次）
            autoSyncLottery(token);
        }
        
        // 自動同步開獎資料
        async function autoSyncLottery(token) {
            const lastSync = localStorage.getItem('lottery_last_sync');
            const today = new Date().toDateString();
            
            // 今天已經同步過就跳過
            if (lastSync === today) {
                console.log('📅 今日已同步過開獎資料');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                console.log('🔄 自動同步開獎資料...');
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/lottery/sync`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    const result = await response.json();
                    localStorage.setItem('lottery_last_sync', today);
                    console.log('✅ 自動同步完成:', result.message);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('⚠️ 自動同步失敗:', e);
            }
        }
        
        function formatMoney(amount) {
            if (amount >= 10000) {
                return (amount / 10000).toFixed(1) + '萬';
            }
            return amount.toLocaleString();
        }
        
        function renderUserList(users) {
            const container = document.getElementById('user-list');
            
            if (!users || users.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">暫無用戶</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            container.innerHTML = users.map(user => `
                <div class="user-item">
                    <div class="user-avatar">
                        ${user.picture_url 
                            ? `<img src="${user.picture_url}" alt="">` 
                            : (user.display_name || '?').charAt(0)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="user-info">
                        <div class="user-name">${user.nickname || user.display_name}</div>
                        <div class="user-role">ID: ${user.id} | 參與 ${user.series_count || 0} 個系列團</div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="role-badge ${user.role === 'admin' ? 'role-admin' : 'role-user'}">
                        ${user.role === 'admin' ? '管理員' : '會員'}
                    </span>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }
        
        function renderSeriesList(series) {
            const container = document.getElementById('series-list');
            
            if (!series || series.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">暫無系列團</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            container.innerHTML = series.map(s => `
                <div class="user-item">
                    <div class="user-avatar">📋</div>
                    <div class="user-info">
                        <div class="user-name">${s.name}</div>
                        <div class="user-role">
                            ${s.member_count} 人 | 
                            ${s.total_periods} 期 | 
                            累計 ${formatMoney(s.total_invested || 0)}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="role-badge ${s.status === 'active' ? 'role-admin' : 'role-user'}">
                        ${s.status === 'active' ? '進行中' : '已結束'}
                    </span>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }
        
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadDashboard);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

                            <p id="jackpot-daily539" class="text-2xl font-bold">800 萬</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="px-6 py-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                        <span class="text-sm text-gray-500">最新開獎</span>
                        <span id="date-daily539" class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">--</span>
                    </div>
                    <div id="numbers-daily539" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
                        <span class="text-gray-400">尚未載入</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 說明區 -->
        <div class="mt-6 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl p-4">
            <div class="flex items-start space-x-3">
                <span class="text-xl">💡</span>
                <div class="text-sm text-amber-800">
                    <p class="font-bold mb-1">使用說明</p>
                    <ul class="list-disc list-inside space-y-1">
                        <li>「立即同步」可抓取最新開獎資訊</li>
                        <li>「匯入歷史」可一次匯入多期歷史記錄</li>
                        <li>資料來源：lotto-8.com（非官方，僅供參考）</li>
                        <li>建議在開獎後 10-30 分鐘再進行同步</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </main>

    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        
        // 檢查登入
        function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = '/';
                return null;
            }
            return token;
        }
        
        // API 請求
        async function apiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
            const token = checkAuth();
            if (!token) return null;
            
            const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
                ...options,
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    ...options.headers
                }
            });
            
            if (response.status === 401) {
                localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
                window.location.href = '/';
                return null;
            }
            
            return response;
        }
        
        // 格式化日期
        function formatDate(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return '--';
            const date = new Date(dateStr);
            const month = date.getMonth() + 1;
            const day = date.getDate();
            const weekdays = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];
            const weekday = weekdays[date.getDay()];
            return `${month}/${day} (${weekday})`;
        }
        
        // 渲染號碼球
        function renderPowerNumbers(numbers) {
            if (!numbers) return '<span class="text-gray-400">無資料</span>';
            
            let html = '';
            if (numbers.first_zone) {
                numbers.first_zone.forEach(n => {
                    html += `<span class="number-ball ball-red">${n.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                });
            }
            if (numbers.second_zone !== undefined) {
                html += `<span class="mx-2 text-gray-400">+</span>`;
                html += `<span class="number-ball ball-yellow">${numbers.second_zone.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
            }
            return html;
        }
        
        function renderSuperNumbers(numbers) {
            if (!numbers) return '<span class="text-gray-400">無資料</span>';
            
            let html = '';
            if (numbers.main) {
                numbers.main.forEach(n => {
                    html += `<span class="number-ball ball-blue">${n.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                });
            }
            if (numbers.special !== undefined) {
                html += `<span class="mx-2 text-gray-400">特</span>`;
                html += `<span class="number-ball ball-yellow">${numbers.special.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
            }
            return html;
        }
        
        function renderDailyNumbers(numbers) {
            if (!numbers) return '<span class="text-gray-400">無資料</span>';
            
            // 處理不同格式
            let numArray = numbers;
            if (numbers.numbers) {
                numArray = numbers.numbers;
            }
            if (!Array.isArray(numArray)) return '<span class="text-gray-400">無資料</span>';
            
            let html = '';
            numArray.forEach(n => {
                html += `<span class="number-ball ball-green">${n.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
            });
            return html;
        }
        
        // 更新卡片資料
        function updateCard(type, data) {
            const jackpotEl = document.getElementById(`jackpot-${type}`);
            const dateEl = document.getElementById(`date-${type}`);
            const numbersEl = document.getElementById(`numbers-${type}`);
            
            if (!data) {
                jackpotEl.textContent = '無法取得';
                dateEl.textContent = '--';
                numbersEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-gray-400">同步失敗</span>';
                return;
            }
            
            if (data.jackpot_display) {
                jackpotEl.textContent = data.jackpot_display;
            }
            
            if (data.latest_draw) {
                dateEl.textContent = formatDate(data.latest_draw.draw_date);
                
                if (type === 'power') {
                    numbersEl.innerHTML = renderPowerNumbers(data.latest_draw.numbers);
                } else if (type === 'super') {
                    numbersEl.innerHTML = renderSuperNumbers(data.latest_draw.numbers);
                } else if (type === 'daily539') {
                    numbersEl.innerHTML = renderDailyNumbers(data.latest_draw.numbers);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // 顯示同步狀態
        function showSyncStatus(success, message) {
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('syncStatus');
            statusEl.classList.remove('hidden', 'bg-green-100', 'text-green-800', 'bg-red-100', 'text-red-800');
            
            if (success) {
                statusEl.classList.add('bg-green-100', 'text-green-800');
                statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="font-bold">✓ 同步成功！</span> ${message}`;
            } else {
                statusEl.classList.add('bg-red-100', 'text-red-800');
                statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="font-bold">✗ 同步失敗</span> ${message}`;
            }
        }
        
        // 顯示匯入狀態
        function showImportStatus(success, message) {
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('importStatus');
            statusEl.classList.remove('hidden', 'bg-green-100', 'text-green-800', 'bg-red-100', 'text-red-800', 'bg-yellow-100', 'text-yellow-800');
            
            if (success) {
                statusEl.classList.add('bg-green-100', 'text-green-800');
                statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="font-bold">✓ 匯入成功！</span> ${message}`;
            } else {
                statusEl.classList.add('bg-red-100', 'text-red-800');
                statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="font-bold">✗ 匯入失敗</span> ${message}`;
            }
        }
        
        // 同步開獎資料
        async function syncLotteryData() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('syncBtn');
            const icon = document.getElementById('syncIcon');
            const text = document.getElementById('syncText');
            
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.classList.add('opacity-50', 'cursor-not-allowed');
            icon.innerHTML = '<svg class="w-5 h-5 spin inline" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>';
            text.textContent = '同步中...';
            
            try {
                const response = await apiRequest('/lottery/sync', { method: 'POST' });
                
                if (!response) return;
                
                const result = await response.json();
                
                if (result.success) {
                    showSyncStatus(true, result.message);
                    document.getElementById('lastSync').textContent = 
                        `上次同步：${new Date(result.updated_at).toLocaleString('zh-TW')}`;
                    await loadLotteryData();
                    await loadDbStats();
                } else {
                    showSyncStatus(false, result.message);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('同步失敗:', error);
                showSyncStatus(false, error.message);
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.classList.remove('opacity-50', 'cursor-not-allowed');
                icon.textContent = '🔄';
                text.textContent = '立即同步';
            }
        }
        
        // 匯入歷史資料
        async function importHistoryData() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('importBtn');
            const icon = document.getElementById('importIcon');
            const text = document.getElementById('importText');
            const limit = document.getElementById('importLimit').value;
            
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.classList.add('opacity-50', 'cursor-not-allowed');
            icon.innerHTML = '<svg class="w-5 h-5 spin inline" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>';
            text.textContent = '匯入中...';
            
            try {
                const response = await apiRequest(`/lottery/import-history?limit=${limit}`, { method: 'POST' });
                
                if (!response) return;
                
                const result = await response.json();
                
                if (result.success) {
                    showImportStatus(true, result.message);
                    await loadDbStats();
                } else {
                    showImportStatus(false, result.message);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('匯入失敗:', error);
                showImportStatus(false, error.message);
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.classList.remove('opacity-50', 'cursor-not-allowed');
                icon.textContent = '📥';
                text.textContent = '匯入歷史';
            }
        }
        
        // 載入資料庫統計
        async function loadDbStats() {
            try {
                // 分別取得各彩種的歷史記錄數量
                for (const type of ['power', 'super', 'daily539']) {
                    const response = await apiRequest(`/lottery/history/${type}?limit=1`);
                    if (response) {
                        const data = await response.json();
                        document.getElementById(`stat-${type}`).textContent = 
                            data.total_count || 0;
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('載入統計失敗:', error);
            }
        }
        
        // 載入開獎資料
        async function loadLotteryData() {
            try {
                const response = await apiRequest('/lottery/latest');
                if (!response) return;
                
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.lotteries) {
                    updateCard('power', data.lotteries.power);
                    updateCard('super', data.lotteries.super);
                    updateCard('daily539', data.lotteries.daily539);
                }
                
                document.getElementById('lastSync').textContent = 
                    `資料時間：${new Date(data.updated_at).toLocaleString('zh-TW')}`;
                    
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('載入失敗:', error);
            }
        }
        
        // 頁面載入
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            checkAuth();
            loadLotteryData();
            loadDbStats();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <!-- 首次登入歡迎彈窗 -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="welcome-modal" style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6); align-items: center; justify-content: center; z-index: 1100;">
        <div style="background: #fff; border-radius: 24px; width: 90%; max-width: 380px; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);">
            <div style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #F26522, #ff8a50); padding: 32px 24px; text-align: center; color: white;">
                <div style="font-size: 48px; margin-bottom: 12px;">🎉</div>

            </div>
            <div style="padding: 24px;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <label style="display: block; font-size: 13px; color: #666; margin-bottom: 6px;">LINE 名稱</label>
                    <input type="text" id="welcome-display-name" disabled style="width: 100%; padding: 12px 14px; border: 1px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 15px; background: #f5f5f5; color: #999;">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 24px;">
                    <label style="display: block; font-size: 13px; color: #666; margin-bottom: 6px;">設定暱稱 <span style="color: #999;">(選填)</span></label>
                    <input type="text" id="welcome-nickname" placeholder="輸入您想使用的暱稱" maxlength="50" style="width: 100%; padding: 12px 14px; border: 1px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 15px;">
                    <p style="font-size: 12px; color: #999; margin-top: 6px;">留空將使用 LINE 名稱</p>
                </div>

                    <button onclick="skipWelcome()" style="flex: 1; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #e0e0e0; background: #fff; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 14px; cursor: pointer;">稍後設定</button>
                    <button onclick="saveWelcomeNickname()" style="flex: 1; padding: 14px; border: none; background: #F26522; color: #fff; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;">確認</button>
                </div>
            </div>

                <p style="font-size: 12px; color: #999;">💡 之後可在「設定」頁面修改</p>

    <!-- 加入集資彈窗 -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="join-modal" style="display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); align-items: center; justify-content: center; z-index: 1000;">
        <div style="background: #fff; border-radius: 20px; width: 90%; max-width: 400px; padding: 24px;">
            <h3 style="margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 18px;">使用邀請碼加入</h3>
            <input type="text" id="invite-code" placeholder="輸入邀請碼" style="width: 100%; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 10px; font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 12px; text-transform: uppercase;">
            <input type="number" id="join-pool" value="500" placeholder="初始份額" style="width: 100%; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 10px; font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px;">
                <button onclick="closeJoinModal()" style="flex: 1; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); background: #fff; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 14px; cursor: pointer;">取消</button>
                <button onclick="joinSeries()" style="flex: 1; padding: 14px; border: none; background: var(--sela-orange); color: #fff; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;">加入</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="toast" style="position: fixed; bottom: 20px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(100px); background: #333; color: #fff; padding: 14px 24px; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 14px; opacity: 0; transition: all 0.3s; z-index: 2000;"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        
        function showJoinModal() { document.getElementById('join-modal').style.display = 'flex'; }
        function closeJoinModal() { document.getElementById('join-modal').style.display = 'none'; }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.style.background = type === 'error' ? '#DC2626' : type === 'success' ? '#16A34A' : '#333';
            toast.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(0)';
            toast.style.opacity = '1';
            setTimeout(() => { toast.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(100px)'; toast.style.opacity = '0'; }, 3000);
        }
        
        async function joinSeries() {
            const code = document.getElementById('invite-code').value.trim().toUpperCase();
            const pool = parseFloat(document.getElementById('join-pool').value);
            if (!code) { showToast('請輸入邀請碼', 'error'); return; }
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
                const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/series/join`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ invitation_code: code, initial_pool_share: pool })
                });
                if (!res.ok) { const err = await res.json(); throw new Error(err.detail || '加入失敗'); }
                closeJoinModal();
                showToast('成功加入集資!', 'success');
                loadDashboardData();
            } catch (e) { showToast(e.message, 'error'); }
        }
        
        // ===== 首次登入相關函數 =====
        function checkFirstLogin(user) {
            const isNewUser = localStorage.getItem('is_new_user');
            if (isNewUser === '1') {
                localStorage.removeItem('is_new_user');
                showWelcomeModal(user);
            }
        }
        
        function showWelcomeModal(user) {
            document.getElementById('welcome-display-name').value = user.display_name;
            document.getElementById('welcome-nickname').value = '';
            document.getElementById('welcome-modal').style.display = 'flex';
            setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('welcome-nickname').focus(), 300);
        }
        
        function closeWelcomeModal() {
            document.getElementById('welcome-modal').style.display = 'none';
        }
        
        function skipWelcome() {
            closeWelcomeModal();
            showToast('歡迎加入!隨時可在設定中修改暱稱', 'success');
        }
        
        async function saveWelcomeNickname() {
            const nickname = document.getElementById('welcome-nickname').value.trim();
            if (!nickname) { skipWelcome(); return; }
            
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
                const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/users/me`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ nickname: nickname })
                });
                if (!res.ok) { const err = await res.json(); throw new Error(err.detail || '儲存失敗'); }
                
                const updatedUser = await res.json();
                const displayName = updatedUser.nickname || updatedUser.display_name;
                document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = displayName;
                document.getElementById('welcome-name').textContent = displayName;
                
                closeWelcomeModal();
                showToast(`歡迎 ${displayName}!祝您好運 🍀`, 'success');
            } catch (e) { showToast(e.message, 'error'); }
        }
        
        // ===== 開獎資訊相關函數 =====
        async function loadLotteryInfo() {
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
                const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/lottery/latest`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                
                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('無法取得開獎資訊');
                
                const data = await res.json();
                renderLotteryCards(data.lotteries);
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load lottery info:', e);
                document.querySelectorAll('.lottery-card').forEach(card => {
                    card.classList.remove('loading-card');
                    card.classList.add('no-data');
                    card.querySelector('.lottery-numbers').innerHTML = '暫無資料';
                });
            }
        }
        
        function renderLotteryCards(lotteries) {
            const container = document.getElementById('lottery-cards');
            
            const powerCard = container.querySelector('.lottery-card.power');
            if (lotteries?.power) {
                renderLotteryCard(powerCard, lotteries.power, 'power');
            } else {
                powerCard.classList.remove('loading-card');
                powerCard.classList.add('no-data');
                powerCard.querySelector('.lottery-numbers').innerHTML = '暫無資料';
            }
            
            const superCard = container.querySelector('.lottery-card.super');
            if (lotteries?.super) {
                renderLotteryCard(superCard, lotteries.super, 'super');
            } else {
                superCard.classList.remove('loading-card');
                superCard.classList.add('no-data');
                superCard.querySelector('.lottery-numbers').innerHTML = '暫無資料';
            }
        }
        
        function renderLotteryCard(cardEl, data, type) {
            cardEl.classList.remove('loading-card');
            
            const draw = data.latest_draw;
            if (!draw) {
                cardEl.classList.add('no-data');
                cardEl.querySelector('.lottery-numbers').innerHTML = '暫無資料';
                return;
            }
            
            const headerEl = cardEl.querySelector('.lottery-card-header');
            const existingDate = headerEl.querySelector('.lottery-date');
            if (existingDate) existingDate.remove();
            
            const dateEl = document.createElement('span');
            dateEl.className = 'lottery-date';
            dateEl.textContent = formatLotteryDate(draw.draw_date);
            headerEl.appendChild(dateEl);
            
            const numbersEl = cardEl.querySelector('.lottery-numbers');
            numbersEl.innerHTML = renderLotteryNumbers(draw.numbers, type);
            
            if (data.jackpot_display) {
                const oldJackpot = cardEl.querySelector('.lottery-jackpot');
                if (oldJackpot) oldJackpot.remove();
                
                const jackpotHtml = `
                    <div class="lottery-jackpot">
                        <span class="jackpot-label">💰 頭獎</span>
                        <span class="jackpot-amount">${data.jackpot_display}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
                cardEl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', jackpotHtml);
            }
        }
        
        function renderLotteryNumbers(numbers, type) {
            let html = '';
            
            if (type === 'power') {
                if (numbers.first_zone) {
                    numbers.first_zone.forEach(num => {
                        html += `<span class="lottery-ball">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                    });
                }
                if (numbers.second_zone !== undefined && numbers.second_zone !== null) {
                    html += `<span class="lottery-divider"></span>`;
                    html += `<span class="lottery-ball second-zone">${String(numbers.second_zone).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                }
            } else if (type === 'super') {
                if (numbers.main) {
                    numbers.main.forEach(num => {
                        html += `<span class="lottery-ball">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                    });
                }
                if (numbers.special !== undefined && numbers.special !== null) {
                    html += `<span class="lottery-divider"></span>`;
                    html += `<span class="lottery-ball special">${String(numbers.special).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                }
            } else {
                if (numbers.numbers || numbers.main) {
                    (numbers.numbers || numbers.main).forEach(num => {
                        html += `<span class="lottery-ball">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                    });
                }
            }
            
            return html || '暫無資料';
        }
        
        function formatLotteryDate(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return '';
            try {
                const parts = dateStr.split(/[\/\-]/);
                if (parts.length >= 3) {
                    return `${parts[1]}/${parts[2]}`;
                }
                return dateStr;
            } catch (e) {
                return dateStr;
            }
        }
        
        // ===== 主要邏輯 =====
        async function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) { window.location.href = '/'; return; }
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/users/me`, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Unauthorized');
                const user = await response.json();
                updateUI(user);
                checkFirstLogin(user);
                loadLotteryInfo();
                loadDashboardData();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Auth error:', error);
                localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
                window.location.href = '/';
            }
        }
        
        function updateUI(user) {
            const displayName = user.nickname || user.display_name;
            document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = displayName;
            document.getElementById('welcome-name').textContent = displayName;
            const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
            if (user.picture_url) { avatarEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${user.picture_url}" alt="avatar">`; }
            else { avatarEl.textContent = displayName.charAt(0); }
            if (user.role === 'admin') { document.getElementById('admin-btn').style.display = 'block'; }
            document.getElementById('loading-overlay').classList.add('hidden');
        }
        
        async function loadDashboardData() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            try {
                const seriesRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/series`, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } });
                if (seriesRes.ok) {
                    const seriesList = await seriesRes.json();
                    let totalPool = 0, totalPrize = 0;
                    seriesList.forEach(s => { totalPool += Number(s.my_pool_share) || 0; });
                    document.getElementById('total-pool').textContent = `$${totalPool.toLocaleString()}`;
                    document.getElementById('series-count').textContent = seriesList.length;
                    document.getElementById('total-prize').textContent = `$${totalPrize.toLocaleString()}`;
                    renderRecentActivity(seriesList);
                }
            } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to load dashboard data:', e); }
        }
        
        async function renderRecentActivity(seriesList) {
            const container = document.getElementById('activity-list');
            if (seriesList.length === 0) return;
            const recentGroups = [];
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            for (const series of seriesList.slice(0, 3)) {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/series/${series.id}/groups`, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } });
                    if (res.ok) {
                        const groups = await res.json();
                        groups.slice(0, 2).forEach(g => { recentGroups.push({ ...g, seriesName: series.name, seriesId: series.id }); });
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to load groups:', e); }
            }
            if (recentGroups.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">📋</div><h3 class="empty-title">尚無單期團</h3><p class="empty-desc">管理員開新期後會顯示在這裡</p></div>`;
                return;
            }
            const statusText = { collecting: '集資中', locked: '已鎖定', purchased: '已購買', drawn: '已開獎', settled: '已結算' };
            container.innerHTML = recentGroups.slice(0, 5).map(g => `
                <a href="/group/${g.id}" class="activity-item">
                    <div class="activity-info">
                        <div class="activity-name">${g.seriesName} - 第 ${g.period_number} 期</div>
                        <div class="activity-detail">${g.lottery_type_name} · 池 $${Number(g.total_pool).toLocaleString()}</div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="activity-status ${g.status}">${statusText[g.status] || g.status}</span>
                </a>
            `).join('');
        }
        
        function logout() { if (confirm('確定要登出嗎?')) { localStorage.removeItem('access_token'); window.location.href = '/'; } }
        
        document.getElementById('join-modal').addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === this) closeJoinModal(); });
        document.getElementById('welcome-modal').addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === this) closeWelcomeModal(); });
        
        checkAuth();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <div class="modal-overlay" id="purchase-modal">
        <div class="modal">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h2 class="modal-title">記錄購買</h2>
                <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal('purchase-modal')">✕</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">實際購買金額(元)</label>
                    <input type="number" class="form-input" id="purchase-amount" min="0">
                    <p class="form-hint">資金池總額:$<span id="pool-amount"></span></p>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">購買注數</label>
                    <input type="number" class="form-input" id="purchase-tickets" value="1" min="1">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal('purchase-modal')">取消</button>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="recordPurchase()">確認</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Settlement Modal -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="settlement-modal">
        <div class="modal" style="max-width: 600px;">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h2 class="modal-title">結算確認</h2>
                <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal('settlement-modal')">✕</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body" id="settlement-body">
                <div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal('settlement-modal')">取消</button>
                <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="executeSettlement()">確認結算</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        const groupId = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop();
        let groupData = null;
        let ticketsData = [];
        let isAdmin = false;
        let lotteryTypeConfig = null;
        
        const LOTTERY_CONFIGS = {
            power: { name: '威力彩', firstZone: 6, firstMax: 38, secondZone: true, secondMax: 8, price: 100 },
            super: { name: '大樂透', firstZone: 6, firstMax: 49, secondZone: true, secondMax: 49, price: 50, secondLabel: '特別號' },
            daily539: { name: '今彩539', firstZone: 5, firstMax: 39, secondZone: false, price: 50 }
        };
        
        async function apiGet(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'Error');
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiPost(url, data) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'Error');
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function loadData() {
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
                if (!token) {
                    window.location.href = '/';
                    return;
                }
                
                groupData = await apiGet(`/groups/${groupId}`);
                ticketsData = await apiGet(`/groups/${groupId}/tickets`);
                
                // Check admin status
                const series = await apiGet(`/series/${groupData.series_id}`);
                isAdmin = series.my_role === 'admin';
                
                // Setup lottery config
                lotteryTypeConfig = LOTTERY_CONFIGS[groupData.lottery_type_code] || LOTTERY_CONFIGS.power;
                
                // Update back button
                document.getElementById('back-btn').href = `/series/${groupData.series_id}`;
                document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = `第 ${groupData.period_number} 期`;
                
                renderContent();
                
            } catch (e) {
                document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = `
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <div class="empty-icon">❌</div>
                        <p class="empty-text">載入失敗:${e.message}</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }
        
        function renderContent() {
            const g = groupData;
            const config = lotteryTypeConfig;
            const statusSteps = ['collecting', 'locked', 'purchased', 'drawn', 'settled'];
            const currentStep = statusSteps.indexOf(g.status);
            
            const content = document.getElementById('content');
            content.innerHTML = `
                <!-- Progress Steps -->
                <div class="progress-steps">
                    ${['集資中', '已鎖定', '已購買', '已開獎', '已結算'].map((label, i) => `
                        <div class="step ${i < currentStep ? 'completed' : ''} ${i === currentStep ? 'active' : ''}">
                            <div class="step-circle">${i < currentStep ? '✓' : i + 1}</div>
                            <div class="step-label">${label}</div>
                        </div>
                    `).join('')}
                </div>
                
                <!-- Status Card -->
                <div class="status-card">
                    <div class="status-header">
                        <div class="status-title">${config.name} 第 ${g.period_number} 期</div>
                        <span class="status-badge status-${g.status}">${getStatusText(g.status)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="status-info">
                        ${g.draw_term ? `<span>📅 台彩期數:${g.draw_term}</span>` : ''}
                        ${g.draw_date ? `<span>🗓️ 開獎日:${g.draw_date}</span>` : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="status-stats">
                        <div class="stat">
                            <div class="stat-label">資金池</div>
                            <div class="stat-value orange">$${Number(g.total_pool).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="stat">
                            <div class="stat-label">已花費</div>
                            <div class="stat-value purple">$${Number(g.total_spent).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="stat">
                            <div class="stat-label">彩券數</div>
                            <div class="stat-value">${ticketsData.length}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="stat">
                            <div class="stat-label">中獎金額</div>
                            <div class="stat-value green">$${Number(g.total_prize).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    ${g.winning_numbers ? renderWinningNumbers(g.winning_numbers) : ''}
                </div>
                
                <!-- Admin Actions -->
                ${isAdmin ? renderAdminActions(g.status) : ''}
                
                <!-- Tickets Section -->
                <div class="section">
                    <div class="section-header">
                        <div class="section-title">🎫 彩券列表 (${ticketsData.length})</div>
                        ${isAdmin && ['locked', 'purchased'].includes(g.status) ? `
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showTicketModal()">+ 新增</button>
                        ` : ''}
                    </div>
                    ${ticketsData.length > 0 ? `
                        <div class="ticket-list">
                            ${ticketsData.map((t, i) => renderTicket(t, i + 1)).join('')}
                        </div>
                    ` : `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">🎫</div>
                            <p class="empty-text">還沒有彩券</p>
                        </div>
                    `}
                </div>
                
                <!-- Contributions Section -->
                ${g.contributions && g.contributions.length > 0 ? `
                    <div class="section">
                        <div class="section-header">
                            <div class="section-title">👥 成員貢獻</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="contrib-list">
                            ${g.contributions.map(c => `
                                <div class="contrib-item">
                                    <span class="contrib-name">${c.display_name || '成員'}</span>
                                    <div>
                                        <span class="contrib-share">$${Number(c.pool_share_at_lock).toLocaleString()}</span>
                                        ${c.prize_share > 0 ? `<span class="contrib-prize"> → +$${Number(c.prize_share).toLocaleString()}</span>` : ''}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ` : ''}
            `;
            
            setupNumberInputs();
        }
        
        function renderAdminActions(status) {
            const actions = {
                collecting: `<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="lockCollection()">🔒 鎖定集資</button>`,
                locked: `
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showPurchaseModal()">🛒 記錄購買</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showTicketModal()">🎫 新增彩券</button>
                `,
                purchased: `
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showDrawModal()">🎰 輸入開獎號碼</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showTicketModal()">🎫 新增彩券</button>
                `,
                drawn: `
                    <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="checkTickets()">🔍 對獎</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showSettlementModal()">💰 結算</button>
                `,
                settled: ''
            };
            
            if (!actions[status]) return '';
            
            return `
                <div class="action-bar">
                    ${actions[status]}
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function renderWinningNumbers(numbers) {
            const config = lotteryTypeConfig;
            let html = '<div class="winning-numbers"><div class="winning-label">開獎號碼</div><div class="number-balls">';
            
            // First zone
            const firstZone = numbers.first_zone || numbers.main || [];
            firstZone.forEach(n => {
                html += `<span class="ball">${n}</span>`;
            });
            
            // Second zone / Special
            if (numbers.second_zone !== undefined) {
                html += `<span class="ball second">${numbers.second_zone}</span>`;
            } else if (numbers.special !== undefined) {
                html += `<span class="ball special">${numbers.special}</span>`;
            }
            
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }
        
        function renderTicket(ticket, index) {
            const numbers = ticket.numbers || {};
            const firstZone = numbers.first_zone || numbers.main || [];
            const hasPrize = ticket.prize_amount > 0;
            const prizeResults = ticket.prize_results || [];
            const matchedNums = new Set();
            
            // Extract matched numbers from prize results
            if (groupData.winning_numbers) {
                const winning = groupData.winning_numbers;
                const winningFirst = winning.first_zone || winning.main || [];
                firstZone.forEach(n => {
                    if (winningFirst.includes(n)) matchedNums.add(n);
                });
            }
            
            return `
                <div class="ticket-item">
                    <div class="ticket-header">
                        <span class="ticket-id">彩券 #${index}</span>
                        ${groupData.status === 'drawn' || groupData.status === 'settled' ? `
                            <span class="ticket-prize ${hasPrize ? 'winner' : 'no-prize'}">
                                ${hasPrize ? `🎉 $${Number(ticket.prize_amount).toLocaleString()}` : '未中獎'}
                            </span>
                        ` : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="ticket-numbers">
                        ${firstZone.map(n => `
                            <span class="ticket-ball ${matchedNums.has(n) ? 'match' : ''}">${n}</span>
                        `).join('')}
                        ${numbers.second_zone !== undefined ? `
                            <span class="ticket-ball ${numbers.second_zone === (groupData.winning_numbers?.second_zone) ? 'special-match' : ''}">${numbers.second_zone}</span>
                        ` : ''}
                        ${numbers.special !== undefined ? `
                            <span class="ticket-ball ${numbers.special === (groupData.winning_numbers?.special) ? 'special-match' : ''}">${numbers.special}</span>
                        ` : ''}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function getStatusText(status) {
            const map = {
                collecting: '集資中',
                locked: '已鎖定',
                purchased: '已購買',
                drawn: '已開獎',
                settled: '已結算'
            };
            return map[status] || status;
        }
        
        function setupNumberInputs() {
            const config = lotteryTypeConfig;
            
            // Ticket modal inputs
            const firstZoneContainer = document.getElementById('first-zone-inputs');
            firstZoneContainer.innerHTML = Array(config.firstZone).fill(0).map((_, i) => 
                `<input type="number" class="number-input first-zone-num" min="1" max="${config.firstMax}" placeholder="${i+1}">`
            ).join('');
            document.getElementById('first-zone-hint').textContent = `選 ${config.firstZone} 個號碼,範圍 1-${config.firstMax}`;
            
            // Second zone
            const secondGroup = document.getElementById('second-zone-group');
            if (config.secondZone) {
                secondGroup.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('second-zone-input').max = config.secondMax;
            } else {
                secondGroup.style.display = 'none';
            }
            
            // Default cost
            document.getElementById('ticket-cost').value = config.price;
            
            // Draw modal inputs
            const drawFirstZone = document.getElementById('draw-first-zone');
            drawFirstZone.innerHTML = Array(config.firstZone).fill(0).map((_, i) => 
                `<input type="number" class="number-input draw-first-num" min="1" max="${config.firstMax}">`
            ).join('');
            
            const drawSecondGroup = document.getElementById('draw-second-zone-group');
            if (config.secondZone) {
                drawSecondGroup.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('draw-second-zone').max = config.secondMax;
                document.getElementById('draw-second-label').textContent = 
                    config.secondLabel || (groupData.lottery_type_code === 'power' ? '第二區' : '特別號');
            } else {
                drawSecondGroup.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }
        
        // Modal Functions
        function showTicketModal() {
            document.getElementById('ticket-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showDrawModal() {
            document.getElementById('draw-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showPurchaseModal() {
            document.getElementById('pool-amount').textContent = Number(groupData.total_pool).toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById('purchase-amount').value = groupData.total_pool;
            document.getElementById('purchase-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        async function showSettlementModal() {
            document.getElementById('settlement-modal').classList.add('active');
            const body = document.getElementById('settlement-body');
            body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            
            try {
                const preview = await apiGet(`/groups/${groupId}/settlement-preview`);
                
                body.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="settlement-preview">
                        <div class="settlement-summary">
                            <div class="settlement-item">
                                <div class="settlement-label">資金池</div>
                                <div class="settlement-value" style="color: var(--sela-orange);">$${Number(preview.total_pool).toLocaleString()}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="settlement-item">
                                <div class="settlement-label">已花費</div>
                                <div class="settlement-value">$${Number(preview.total_spent).toLocaleString()}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="settlement-item">
                                <div class="settlement-label">中獎金額(稅後)</div>
                                <div class="settlement-value" style="color: var(--success);">$${Number(preview.total_prize_after_tax).toLocaleString()}</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <h4 style="margin-bottom: 12px; font-size: 14px;">成員結算明細</h4>
                        <div style="max-height: 300px; overflow-y: auto;">
                            ${preview.members.map(m => `
                                <div class="contrib-item">
                                    <span class="contrib-name">${m.display_name}</span>
                                    <div style="text-align: right; font-size: 13px;">
                                        <div>原份額: $${Number(m.pool_share_before).toLocaleString()}</div>
                                        <div style="color: var(--success);">獎金: +$${Number(m.prize_share).toLocaleString()}</div>
                                        <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--sela-orange);">新份額: $${Number(m.pool_share_after).toLocaleString()}</div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            } catch (e) {
                body.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><p class="empty-text">${e.message}</p></div>`;
            }
        }
        
        function closeModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('active');
        }
        
        // API Actions
        async function lockCollection() {
            if (!confirm('確定要鎖定集資嗎?鎖定後成員無法再調整份額。')) return;
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/lock`, {});
                showToast('已鎖定集資', 'success');
                loadData();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function recordPurchase() {
            const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('purchase-amount').value);
            const tickets = parseInt(document.getElementById('purchase-tickets').value);
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/purchase?total_spent=${amount}&total_tickets=${tickets}`, {});
                closeModal('purchase-modal');
                showToast('已記錄購買', 'success');
                loadData();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function addTicket() {
            const config = lotteryTypeConfig;
            const firstZone = [...document.querySelectorAll('.first-zone-num')].map(el => parseInt(el.value)).filter(n => n);
            const betCount = parseInt(document.getElementById('bet-count').value);
            const cost = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ticket-cost').value);
            
            if (firstZone.length !== config.firstZone) {
                showToast(`請輸入 ${config.firstZone} 個號碼`, 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            const numbers = { first_zone: firstZone };
            
            if (config.secondZone) {
                const secondZone = parseInt(document.getElementById('second-zone-input').value);
                if (!secondZone) {
                    showToast('請輸入特別號', 'error');
                    return;
                }
                if (groupData.lottery_type_code === 'power') {
                    numbers.second_zone = secondZone;
                } else {
                    numbers.special = secondZone;
                }
            }
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/tickets`, {
                    numbers,
                    bet_count: betCount,
                    cost
                });
                closeModal('ticket-modal');
                showToast('彩券已新增', 'success');
                loadData();
                
                // Clear inputs
                document.querySelectorAll('.first-zone-num').forEach(el => el.value = '');
                document.getElementById('second-zone-input').value = '';
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function inputDrawResult() {
            const config = lotteryTypeConfig;
            const firstZone = [...document.querySelectorAll('.draw-first-num')].map(el => parseInt(el.value)).filter(n => n);
            
            if (firstZone.length !== config.firstZone) {
                showToast(`請輸入 ${config.firstZone} 個開獎號碼`, 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            const winningNumbers = {};
            
            if (groupData.lottery_type_code === 'power') {
                winningNumbers.first_zone = firstZone;
                const secondZone = parseInt(document.getElementById('draw-second-zone').value);
                if (config.secondZone && !secondZone) {
                    showToast('請輸入第二區號碼', 'error');
                    return;
                }
                winningNumbers.second_zone = secondZone;
            } else if (groupData.lottery_type_code === 'super') {
                winningNumbers.main = firstZone;
                const special = parseInt(document.getElementById('draw-second-zone').value);
                if (!special) {
                    showToast('請輸入特別號', 'error');
                    return;
                }
                winningNumbers.special = special;
            } else {
                winningNumbers.main = firstZone;
            }
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/draw`, { winning_numbers: winningNumbers });
                closeModal('draw-modal');
                showToast('開獎號碼已輸入', 'success');
                loadData();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function checkTickets() {
            try {
                const result = await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/check-tickets`, {});
                showToast(`對獎完成!總獎金 $${Number(result.total_prize).toLocaleString()}`, 'success');
                loadData();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function executeSettlement() {
            if (!confirm('確定要執行結算嗎?結算後將更新所有成員的份額。')) return;
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/groups/${groupId}/settle`, {});
                closeModal('settlement-modal');
                showToast('結算完成!', 'success');
                loadData();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type} show`;
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }
        
        // Modal close on overlay click
        document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(overlay => {
            overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active');
            });
        });
        
        loadData();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-TW">

    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes">
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="default">
    <title>SELA 樂透一路發</title>
    <link rel="icon" type="image/jpeg" href="/static/logo.jpg">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">

    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Noto+Sans+TC:wght@400;500;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }

            --sela-orange: #F26522;
            --sela-orange-dark: #D85A1E;
        }

        }
        
        .title {

        }
        
        .subtitle {
            font-size: 16px;
            color: #8B7355;
            margin-bottom: 48px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        
        .login-content {
            width: 100%;
            text-align: center;
        }
        
        .login-btn {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            gap: 12px;
            width: 100%;
            padding: 16px 24px;
            background: #06C755;
            color: white;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 14px;
            font-size: 17px;
            font-weight: 700;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
            text-decoration: none;
            box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(6, 199, 85, 0.35);
        }
        
        .login-btn:hover {

    <script>
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const accessToken = urlParams.get('token');
        const isNew = urlParams.get('new');
        const error = urlParams.get('error');
        
        // 顯示錯誤訊息
        if (error) {
            const errorMsg = document.getElementById('error-msg');
            errorMsg.textContent = '登入失敗:' + decodeURIComponent(error);
            errorMsg.style.display = 'block';
            window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/');
        }
        
        // 處理登入成功
        if (accessToken) {
            localStorage.setItem('access_token', accessToken);
            
            // 如果是新用戶，標記到 localStorage
            if (isNew === '1') {
                localStorage.setItem('is_new_user', '1');
            }
            
            // 清除 URL 參數並跳轉
            window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/');
            window.location.href = '/dashboard';
        }
        
        // 如果已有 token，直接跳轉
        const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
        if (token && !error) {
            window.location.href = '/dashboard';
        }
        
        // 點擊登入按鈕時顯示 loading
        document.getElementById('login-btn').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            document.getElementById('login-content').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('loading').style.display = 'flex';
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
