    <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        const seriesId = window.location.pathname.split('/').pop();
        let seriesData = null;
        let currentSeries = null; // Alias for member-requests.js
        let lotteryTypes = [];
        let isAdmin = false;
        let currentInvitation = null;
        
        // Phase 1: 異動申請相關變數
        let pendingRequestsCount = 0;
        let myPendingRequest = null;
        let currentRejectRequestId = null;
        
        async function apiGet(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'Error');
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiPost(url, data) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'Error');
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function loadData() {
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
                if (!token) {
                    window.location.href = '/';
                    return;
                }
                
                [seriesData, lotteryTypes] = await Promise.all([
                    apiGet(`/series/${seriesId}`),
                    apiGet('/lottery-types')
                ]);
                
                currentSeries = seriesData; // Alias
                isAdmin = seriesData.my_role === 'admin';
                renderContent();
                
                // Phase 1: 初始化異動功能
                initPhase1();
                
            } catch (e) {
                document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = `
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <div class="empty-icon">❌</div>
                        <p class="empty-text">載入失敗:${e.message}</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }
        
        async function loadSeriesDetail() {
            seriesData = await apiGet(`/series/${seriesId}`);
            currentSeries = seriesData;
            renderContent();
        }
        
        function renderContent() {
            const s = seriesData;
            const content = document.getElementById('content');
            
            // 判斷是否為彈性模式 (後端欄位: withdrawal_policy)
            // flexible = 彈性模式, no_withdraw = 死戰到底
            const isFlexibleMode = s.withdrawal_policy === 'flexible';
            const modeText = isFlexibleMode ? '🔄 彈性模式' : '🔒 死戰到底';
            const modeClass = isFlexibleMode ? 'mode-flexible' : 'mode-fixed';
            
            content.innerHTML = `
                <!-- Info Card -->
                <div class="info-card">
                    <div class="info-header">
                        <div>
                            <h2 class="info-title">${s.name}</h2>
                            <span class="mode-badge ${modeClass}">${modeText}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span class="status-badge ${s.status === 'active' ? 'status-active' : 'status-ended'}">
                            ${s.status === 'active' ? '進行中' : '已結束'}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="info-stats">
                        <div class="info-stat">
                            <div class="info-stat-label">成員數</div>
                            <div class="info-stat-value">${s.member_count}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="info-stat">
                            <div class="info-stat-label">資金池</div>
                            <div class="info-stat-value orange">$${Number(s.current_pool).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="info-stat">
                            <div class="info-stat-label">已開期數</div>
                            <div class="info-stat-value">${s.total_periods}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="info-stat">
                            <div class="info-stat-label">我的份額</div>
                            <div class="info-stat-value green">$${Number(s.my_pool_share).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    ${s.description ? `<p class="info-desc">${s.description}</p>` : ''}
                </div>
                
                <!-- 我的申請狀態 (Phase 1) -->
                <div id="my-request-status"></div>
                
                ${isAdmin ? `
                <div class="admin-actions">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showNewPeriodModal()">🎰 開新期</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showInviteModal()">🎫 產生邀請碼</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showTopupModal()">💰 加碼</button>
                    ${isFlexibleMode ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showReduceModal()">📉 減碼</button>` : ''}
                    ${s.member_count === 1 ? `<button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="confirmDeleteSeries()">🗑️ 刪除集資</button>` : ''}
                </div>
                ` : `
                <div class="member-actions" id="member-action-btns">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showTopupModal()">💰 加碼</button>
                    ${isFlexibleMode ? `
                        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showReduceModal()">📉 申請減碼</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="showWithdrawModal()">🚪 申請退出</button>
                    ` : ''}
                </div>
                `}
                
                <!-- Tabs -->
                <div class="tabs">
                    <div class="tab active" data-tab="periods">單期團記錄</div>
                    <div class="tab" data-tab="members">成員 (${s.member_count})</div>
                    ${isAdmin ? '<div class="tab" data-tab="invites">邀請碼</div>' : ''}
                    ${isAdmin ? '<div class="tab" data-tab="requests">異動申請</div>' : ''}
                </div>
                
                <!-- Tab Contents -->
                <div id="tab-periods" class="tab-content active"></div>
                <div id="tab-members" class="tab-content"></div>
                ${isAdmin ? '<div id="tab-invites" class="tab-content"></div>' : ''}
                ${isAdmin ? '<div id="tab-requests" class="tab-content"></div>' : ''}
            `;
            
            // Setup tab switching
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(tab => {
                tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
                    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    tab.classList.add('active');
                    document.getElementById(`tab-${tab.dataset.tab}`).classList.add('active');
                    
                    // 點擊異動申請 Tab 時載入資料
                    if (tab.dataset.tab === 'requests') {
                        loadRequests();
                    }
                });
            });
            
            // Setup lottery type select
            const select = document.getElementById('lottery-type-select');
            select.innerHTML = (s.allowed_lottery_types || []).map(code => {
                const lt = lotteryTypes.find(t => t.code === code);
                return `<option value="${code}">${lt ? lt.name : code}</option>`;
            }).join('');
            
            loadPeriods();
            loadMembers();
            if (isAdmin) loadInvitations();
        }
        
        async function loadPeriods() {
            const container = document.getElementById('tab-periods');
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            
            try {
                const groups = await apiGet(`/series/${seriesId}/groups`);
                
                if (groups.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="section">
                            <div class="empty-state">
                                <div class="empty-icon">📋</div>
                                <p class="empty-text">還沒有開過期,${isAdmin ? '點擊「開新期」開始' : '等待管理員開新期'}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="section">
                        <div class="group-list">
                            ${groups.map(g => `
                                <a href="/group/${g.id}" class="group-item">
                                    <div class="group-info">
                                        <div class="group-title">
                                            第 ${g.period_number} 期
                                            <span class="group-status ${g.status}">${getStatusText(g.status)}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="group-meta">
                                            <span>🎰 ${g.lottery_type_name}</span>
                                            ${g.draw_term ? `<span>📅 ${g.draw_term}</span>` : ''}
                                            <span>💰 池 $${Number(g.total_pool).toLocaleString()}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    ${g.total_prize > 0 ? `<div class="group-prize">+$${Number(g.total_prize).toLocaleString()}</div>` : '<span style="color: var(--text-secondary);">→</span>'}
                                </a>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            } catch (e) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><p class="empty-text">載入失敗:${e.message}</p></div>`;
            }
        }
        
        async function loadMembers() {
            const container = document.getElementById('tab-members');
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            
            try {
                const members = await apiGet(`/series/${seriesId}/members`);
                
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="section">
                        <div class="member-list">
                            ${members.map(m => `
                                <div class="member-item">
                                    <div class="member-info">
                                        <div class="member-avatar">
                                            ${m.picture_url ? `<img src="${m.picture_url}">` : m.display_name[0]}
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <span class="member-name">${m.display_name}</span>
                                            ${m.role === 'admin' ? '<span class="member-role">管理員</span>' : ''}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="member-share">$${Number(m.pool_share).toLocaleString()}</div>
                                </div>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            } catch (e) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><p class="empty-text">載入失敗</p></div>`;
            }
        }
        
        async function loadInvitations() {
            const container = document.getElementById('tab-invites');
            if (!container) return;
            
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div class="section">
                    <div class="invite-section">
                        ${currentInvitation ? `
                            <div class="invite-code-display">
                                <span class="invite-code">${currentInvitation.code}</span>
                                <button class="copy-btn" onclick="copyCode('${currentInvitation.code}')">複製</button>
                            </div>
                            <p class="invite-info">
                                已使用 ${currentInvitation.used_count}/${currentInvitation.max_uses || '∞'} 次
                                ${currentInvitation.expires_at ? ` · 有效期至 ${new Date(currentInvitation.expires_at).toLocaleDateString()}` : ''}
                            </p>
                        ` : `
                            <div class="empty-state">
                                <div class="empty-icon">🎫</div>
                                <p class="empty-text">點擊上方「產生邀請碼」來建立邀請連結</p>
                            </div>
                        `}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        // ==================== Phase 1: 異動申請功能 ====================
        
        function initPhase1() {
            loadMyRequestStatus();
            if (isAdmin) {
                loadPendingRequestsCount();
            }
        }
        
        async function loadPendingRequestsCount() {
            if (!isAdmin) return;
            
            try {
                const data = await apiGet(`/member-requests/series/${seriesId}`);
                pendingRequestsCount = data.requests.filter(r => r.status === 'pending').length;
                updateRequestsBadge();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load pending requests count:', e);
            }
        }
        
        function updateRequestsBadge() {
            const tab = document.querySelector('[data-tab="requests"]');
            if (tab && pendingRequestsCount > 0) {
                tab.innerHTML = `異動申請 <span class="badge">${pendingRequestsCount}</span>`;
            } else if (tab) {
                tab.innerHTML = '異動申請';
            }
        }
        
        async function loadMyRequestStatus() {
            try {
                const requests = await apiGet(`/member-requests/my?series_id=${seriesId}`);
                myPendingRequest = requests.find(r => r.status === 'pending');
                updateMyRequestUI();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load my requests:', e);
            }
        }
        
        function updateMyRequestUI() {
            const container = document.getElementById('my-request-status');
            if (!container) return;
            
            if (myPendingRequest) {
                const typeText = myPendingRequest.request_type === 'reduce' ? '減碼' : '退出';
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="pending-request-card">
                        <div class="pending-request-info">
                            <span class="pending-badge">⏳ 待審核</span>
                            <span>${typeText}申請</span>
                            ${myPendingRequest.amount ? `<span>$${Number(myPendingRequest.amount).toLocaleString()}</span>` : ''}
                        </div>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary" onclick="cancelMyRequest(${myPendingRequest.id})">取消申請</button>
                    </div>
                `;
                // 隱藏減碼退出按鈕
                const actionBtns = document.getElementById('member-action-btns');
                if (actionBtns) actionBtns.style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                container.innerHTML = '';
                const actionBtns = document.getElementById('member-action-btns');
                if (actionBtns) actionBtns.style.display = 'flex';
            }
        }
        
        async function loadRequests() {
            const container = document.getElementById('tab-requests');
            if (!container) return;
            
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            
            try {
                const data = await apiGet(`/member-requests/series/${seriesId}`);
                
                if (data.requests.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="section">
                            <div class="empty-state">
                                <div class="empty-icon">📋</div>
                                <p class="empty-text">目前沒有異動申請</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="section">
                        <div class="request-list">
                            ${data.requests.map(r => {
                                const typeText = r.request_type === 'reduce' ? '減碼' : '退出';
                                const statusText = r.status === 'pending' ? '待審核' : (r.status === 'approved' ? '已核准' : '已拒絕');
                                return `
                                    <div class="request-item ${r.status}">
                                        <div class="request-header">
                                            <span class="request-user">${r.user_name}</span>
                                            <span class="request-type-badge ${r.request_type}">${typeText}</span>
                                            <span class="request-status-badge ${r.status}">${statusText}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="request-details">
                                            ${r.amount ? `<span>金額: $${Number(r.amount).toLocaleString()}</span>` : '<span>全額退出</span>'}
                                            <span>原份額: $${Number(r.pool_share_before).toLocaleString()}</span>
                                            <span>${new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        ${r.reason ? `<div class="request-reason">原因: ${r.reason}</div>` : ''}
                                        ${r.review_note ? `<div class="request-review-note">審核備註: ${r.review_note}</div>` : ''}
                                        ${r.status === 'pending' ? `
                                            <div class="request-actions">
                                                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-success" onclick="approveRequest(${r.id})">✓ 核准</button>
                                                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="showRejectModal(${r.id})">✕ 拒絕</button>
                                            </div>
                                        ` : ''}
                                    </div>
                                `;
                            }).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            } catch (e) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><p class="empty-text">載入失敗: ${e.message}</p></div>`;
            }
        }
        
        function showReduceModal() {
            // 管理員減碼直接執行，不需檢查 pending request
            const isAdmin = currentSeries?.my_role === 'admin';
            if (!isAdmin && myPendingRequest) {
                showToast('您已有待審核的申請', 'error');
                return;
            }
            const myShare = currentSeries?.my_pool_share || 0;
            const maxReduce = Math.max(0, myShare - 50);  // 至少保留 50 元
            
            // 動態調整標題和按鈕（管理員 vs 成員）
            const modalTitle = document.querySelector('#reduce-modal .modal-title');
            const submitBtn = document.querySelector('#reduce-modal .btn-primary');
            if (isAdmin) {
                modalTitle.textContent = '📉 減碼';
                submitBtn.textContent = '確認減碼';
            } else {
                modalTitle.textContent = '📉 申請減碼';
                submitBtn.textContent = '送出申請';
            }
            
            document.getElementById('reduce-current-share').textContent = `$${Number(myShare).toLocaleString()}`;
            document.getElementById('reduce-max-amount').textContent = `$${Number(maxReduce).toLocaleString()}`;
            document.getElementById('reduce-amount').max = maxReduce;
            document.getElementById('reduce-amount').placeholder = `最多 ${maxReduce} 元`;
            
            document.getElementById('reduce-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showWithdrawModal() {
            if (myPendingRequest) {
                showToast('您已有待審核的申請', 'error');
                return;
            }
            const myShare = currentSeries?.my_pool_share || 0;
            document.getElementById('withdraw-share-display').textContent = `$${Number(myShare).toLocaleString()}`;
            document.getElementById('withdraw-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        async function submitReduceRequest() {
            const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('reduce-amount').value);
            const reason = document.getElementById('reduce-reason').value.trim();
            
            if (!amount || amount <= 0) {
                showToast('請輸入減碼金額', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const result = await apiPost(`/member-requests/series/${seriesId}/reduce`, {
                    amount: amount,
                    reason: reason || null
                });
                
                closeModal('reduce-modal');
                
                // 根據回傳判斷是否直接執行（管理員）或需審核（成員）
                if (result.auto_approved) {
                    showToast(`減碼 $${amount} 已執行`, 'success');
                } else {
                    showToast('減碼申請已送出，等待管理員審核', 'success');
                    loadMyRequestStatus();
                }
                
                loadSeriesDetail();
                
                document.getElementById('reduce-amount').value = '';
                document.getElementById('reduce-reason').value = '';
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function submitWithdrawRequest() {
            const reason = document.getElementById('withdraw-reason').value.trim();
            
            if (!confirm('確定要申請退出此集資嗎?此操作需經管理員審核。')) {
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/member-requests/series/${seriesId}/withdraw`, {
                    reason: reason || null
                });
                
                closeModal('withdraw-modal');
                showToast('退出申請已送出,等待管理員審核', 'success');
                
                loadMyRequestStatus();
                loadSeriesDetail();
                
                document.getElementById('withdraw-reason').value = '';
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function cancelMyRequest(requestId) {
            if (!confirm('確定要取消此申請嗎?')) {
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/member-requests/${requestId}/cancel`, {});
                showToast('申請已取消', 'success');
                myPendingRequest = null;
                updateMyRequestUI();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function approveRequest(requestId) {
            if (!confirm('確定要核准此申請嗎?核准後將立即執行減碼/退出。')) {
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const res = await apiPost(`/member-requests/${requestId}/review`, {
                    approved: true,
                    note: null
                });
                
                showToast(res.message || '已核准', 'success');
                loadRequests();
                loadPendingRequestsCount();
                loadSeriesDetail();
                loadMembers();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        function showRejectModal(requestId) {
            currentRejectRequestId = requestId;
            document.getElementById('reject-note').value = '';
            document.getElementById('reject-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        async function submitReject() {
            const note = document.getElementById('reject-note').value.trim();
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/member-requests/${currentRejectRequestId}/review`, {
                    approved: false,
                    note: note || null
                });
                
                closeModal('reject-modal');
                showToast('已拒絕此申請', 'success');
                loadRequests();
                loadPendingRequestsCount();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        // ==================== 原有功能 ====================
        
        function getStatusText(status) {
            const map = {
                collecting: '集資中',
                locked: '已鎖定',
                purchased: '已購買',
                drawn: '已開獎',
                settled: '已結算'
            };
            return map[status] || status;
        }
        
        function showNewPeriodModal() {
            document.getElementById('new-period-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showInviteModal() {
            document.getElementById('invite-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showTopupModal() {
            document.getElementById('topup-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function closeModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('active');
        }
        
        async function createNewPeriod() {
            const lotteryType = document.getElementById('lottery-type-select').value;
            const drawTerm = document.getElementById('draw-term').value.trim();
            const drawDate = document.getElementById('draw-date').value;
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/series/${seriesId}/groups`, {
                    lottery_type_code: lotteryType,
                    draw_term: drawTerm || null,
                    draw_date: drawDate || null
                });
                
                closeModal('new-period-modal');
                showToast('新期建立成功!', 'success');
                loadPeriods();
                
                document.getElementById('draw-term').value = '';
                document.getElementById('draw-date').value = '';
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function createInvitation() {
            const maxUses = parseInt(document.getElementById('max-uses').value);
            const expiresDays = parseInt(document.getElementById('expires-days').value);
            
            try {
                currentInvitation = await apiPost(`/series/${seriesId}/invitations`, {
                    max_uses: maxUses,
                    expires_in_days: expiresDays
                });
                
                closeModal('invite-modal');
                showToast('邀請碼已產生!', 'success');
                loadInvitations();
                
                document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
                document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                document.querySelector('[data-tab="invites"]').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('tab-invites').classList.add('active');
                
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function topup() {
            const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('topup-amount').value);
            
            if (amount < 50) {
                showToast('加碼金額至少 50 元', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                await apiPost(`/series/${seriesId}/members/me/topup`, { amount });
                
                closeModal('topup-modal');
                showToast(`成功加碼 $${amount}!`, 'success');
                
                seriesData = await apiGet(`/series/${seriesId}`);
                currentSeries = seriesData;
                renderContent();
                initPhase1();
                
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        function copyCode(code) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(() => {
                showToast('邀請碼已複製!', 'success');
            });
        }
        
        async function confirmDeleteSeries() {
            if (!confirm('確定要刪除此集資嗎？此操作無法復原！')) {
                return;
            }
            
            if (!confirm('再次確認：刪除後所有資料將永久消失，確定繼續？')) {
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                await apiDelete(`/series/${seriesId}`);
                showToast('集資已刪除', 'success');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.location.href = '/series';
                }, 1000);
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function apiDelete(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'DELETE',
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                }
            });
            
            if (!response.ok) {
                const err = await response.json();
                throw new Error(err.detail || '操作失敗');
            }
            
            return response.json();
        }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type} show`;
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }
        
        // Modal close on overlay click
        document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(overlay => {
            overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active');
            });
        });
        
        loadData();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

        </div>

            <!-- 按鈕由 JS 動態生成 -->
        </div>

            <div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>

    <!-- Create Modal -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="create-modal">
        <div class="modal">
            <div class="modal-header">

            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <div class="form-group">

                    <input type="number" class="form-input" id="initial-pool" value="500" min="100" step="100">
                    <p class="form-hint" id="pool-hint">請先選擇彩種</p>
                </div>

                    <label class="form-label">運作模式</label>

                        <label class="radio-item">
                            <input type="radio" name="withdrawal_policy" value="flexible" checked>
                            <div class="lottery-info">
                                <span class="lottery-name">🔄 彈性模式</span>
                                <span class="lottery-price">允許成員申請減碼/退出</span>
                            </div>
                        </label>
                        <label class="radio-item">
                            <input type="radio" name="withdrawal_policy" value="no_withdraw">
                            <div class="lottery-info">
                                <span class="lottery-name">🔒 死戰到底</span>
                                <span class="lottery-price">只進不出，中獎才結束</span>
                            </div>
                        </label>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">備註(選填)</label>
                    <textarea class="form-input" id="series-description" rows="3" placeholder="團規說明..."></textarea>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal('create-modal')">取消</button>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="createSeries()">建立</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Join Modal -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="join-modal">
        <div class="modal">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h2 class="modal-title">使用邀請碼加入</h2>
                <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal('join-modal')">✕</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">邀請碼</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-input" id="invite-code" placeholder="輸入 8 位邀請碼" maxlength="8" style="text-transform: uppercase;">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">初始份額(元)</label>
                    <input type="number" class="form-input" id="join-pool" value="500" min="50" step="50">
                    <p class="form-hint">加入時投入的金額</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal('join-modal')">取消</button>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="joinSeries()">加入</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        let lotteryTypes = [];
        let selectedLotteryType = null;
        
        const LOTTERY_PRICES = { 'power': 100, 'super': 50, 'daily539': 50 };
        
        async function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) { window.location.href = '/'; return null; }
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/users/me`, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } });
                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Unauthorized');
                const user = await res.json();
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
                if (user.picture_url) avatarEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${user.picture_url}" alt="">`;
                else avatarEl.textContent = (user.nickname || user.display_name || '?')[0];
                return user;
            } catch (e) { localStorage.removeItem('access_token'); window.location.href = '/'; return null; }
        }
        
        async function apiGet(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error(await res.text());
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiPost(url, data) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            if (!res.ok) { const err = await res.json(); throw new Error(err.detail || 'Error'); }
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function loadLotteryTypes() {
            try {
                lotteryTypes = await apiGet('/lottery-types');
                renderLotteryTypeRadios();
            } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to load lottery types:', e); }
        }
        
        function renderLotteryTypeRadios() {
            const container = document.getElementById('lottery-types');
            container.innerHTML = lotteryTypes.map(lt => `
                <label class="radio-item">
                    <input type="radio" name="lottery_type" value="${lt.code}" onchange="onLotteryTypeChange('${lt.code}')">
                    <div class="lottery-info">
                        <span class="lottery-name">${lt.name}</span>
                        <span class="lottery-price">每注 ${lt.price_per_bet} 元</span>
                    </div>
                </label>
            `).join('');
        }
        
        function onLotteryTypeChange(code) {
            selectedLotteryType = code;
            const price = LOTTERY_PRICES[code] || 50;
            const poolInput = document.getElementById('initial-pool');
            const hintEl = document.getElementById('pool-hint');
            poolInput.min = price;
            poolInput.step = price;
            poolInput.value = price * 5;
            const lt = lotteryTypes.find(t => t.code === code);
            const ltName = lt ? lt.name : code;
            hintEl.textContent = `${ltName}每注 ${price} 元,建議 ${price * 5}~${price * 20} 元`;
        }
        
        async function loadSeriesList() {
            const container = document.getElementById('series-list');
            try {
                const seriesList = await apiGet('/series');
                if (seriesList.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">🎰</div>
                            <h3 class="empty-title">還沒有加入任何集資</h3>
                            <p class="empty-desc">建立一個新的集資,或使用邀請碼加入</p>
                        </div>`;
                    return;
                }
                container.innerHTML = seriesList.map(s => `
                    <a href="/series/${s.id}" class="series-card">
                        <div class="series-header">
                            <div>
                                <div class="series-name">${s.name}${s.my_role === 'admin' ? '<span class="my-role">管理員</span>' : ''}</div>
                                <div class="series-lottery-types">${(s.lottery_types || []).map(lt => `<span class="lottery-tag">${getLotteryTypeName(lt)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
                            </div>
                            <span class="series-status ${s.status === 'active' ? 'status-active' : 'status-ended'}">${s.status === 'active' ? '進行中' : '已結束'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="series-stats">
                            <div class="stat-item"><div class="stat-label">成員數</div><div class="stat-value">${s.member_count}</div></div>
                            <div class="stat-item"><div class="stat-label">資金池</div><div class="stat-value orange">$${Number(s.current_pool).toLocaleString()}</div></div>
                            <div class="stat-item"><div class="stat-label">已開期數</div><div class="stat-value">${s.total_periods}</div></div>
                            <div class="stat-item"><div class="stat-label">我的份額</div><div class="stat-value green">$${Number(s.my_pool_share).toLocaleString()}</div></div>
                        </div>
                    </a>`).join('');
            } catch (e) {
                container.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">❌</div><h3 class="empty-title">載入失敗</h3><p class="empty-desc">${e.message}</p></div>`;
            }
        }
        
        function getLotteryTypeName(code) {
            const lt = lotteryTypes.find(t => t.code === code);
            return lt ? lt.name : code;
        }
        
        function showCreateModal() {
            selectedLotteryType = null;
            document.getElementById('series-name').value = '';
            document.getElementById('series-description').value = '';
            document.getElementById('initial-pool').value = '500';
            document.getElementById('pool-hint').textContent = '請先選擇彩種';
            document.querySelectorAll('input[name="lottery_type"]').forEach(el => el.checked = false);
            document.getElementById('create-modal').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showJoinModal() { document.getElementById('join-modal').classList.add('active'); }
        function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('active'); }
        
        async function createSeries() {
            const name = document.getElementById('series-name').value.trim();
            const initialPool = parseFloat(document.getElementById('initial-pool').value);
            const description = document.getElementById('series-description').value.trim();
            const selectedType = document.querySelector('input[name="lottery_type"]:checked');
            const selectedPolicy = document.querySelector('input[name="withdrawal_policy"]:checked');
            
            if (!name) { showToast('請輸入集資名稱', 'error'); return; }
            if (!selectedType) { showToast('請選擇一種彩種', 'error'); return; }
            
            const lotteryCode = selectedType.value;
            const price = LOTTERY_PRICES[lotteryCode] || 50;
            const withdrawalPolicy = selectedPolicy ? selectedPolicy.value : 'flexible';
            
            if (initialPool < price) { showToast(`初始份額至少 ${price} 元`, 'error'); return; }
            if (initialPool % price !== 0) { showToast(`份額必須是 ${price} 的倍數`, 'error'); return; }
            
            try {
                await apiPost('/series', {
                    name, initial_pool_share: initialPool,
                    description: description || null,
                    allowed_lottery_types: [lotteryCode],
                    withdrawal_policy: withdrawalPolicy
                });
                closeModal('create-modal');
                showToast('集資建立成功!', 'success');
                loadSeriesList();
            } catch (e) { showToast(e.message, 'error'); }
        }
        
        async function joinSeries() {
            const code = document.getElementById('invite-code').value.trim().toUpperCase();
            const initialPool = parseFloat(document.getElementById('join-pool').value);
            
            if (!code || code.length < 6) { showToast('請輸入有效的邀請碼', 'error'); return; }
            if (initialPool < 50) { showToast('初始份額至少 50 元', 'error'); return; }
            
            try {
                await apiPost('/series/join', { invitation_code: code, initial_pool_share: initialPool });
                closeModal('join-modal');
                showToast('成功加入集資!', 'success');
                loadSeriesList();
                document.getElementById('invite-code').value = '';
                document.getElementById('join-pool').value = '500';
            } catch (e) { showToast(e.message, 'error'); }
        }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type} show`;
            setTimeout(() => { toast.classList.remove('show'); }, 3000);
        }
        
        document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(overlay => {
            overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active'); });
        });
        
        async function init() {
            const user = await checkAuth();
            if (user) {
                // 動態生成操作按鈕（建立集資僅限系統管理員）
                const actionBar = document.getElementById('action-bar');
                let buttons = '';
                if (user.is_admin) {
                    buttons += '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="showCreateModal()">➕ 建立集資</button>';
                }
                buttons += '<button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="showJoinModal()">🎫 使用邀請碼加入</button>';
                actionBar.innerHTML = buttons;
                
                await loadLotteryTypes();
                await loadSeriesList();
            }
        }
        
        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<body>
    <header class="header">

                    <input type="text" class="form-input" id="nickname" placeholder="設定您的暱稱" maxlength="50">
                    <p class="form-hint">在系統中顯示的名稱，留空則使用 LINE 名稱</p>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Email</label>
                    <input type="email" class="form-input" id="email" placeholder="選填">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">電話</label>
                    <input type="tel" class="form-input" id="phone" placeholder="選填">
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" id="save-btn" onclick="saveProfile()">
                    💾 儲存變更
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- 通知設定 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-header">
                <span>🔔</span>
                <span>推播通知</span>
            </div>
            
            <!-- 推播狀態 -->
            <div class="menu-item" id="push-status-item">
                <span class="menu-icon push">📱</span>
                <div class="menu-content">
                    <div class="menu-title">推播通知</div>
                    <div class="menu-desc" id="push-desc">檢查中...</div>
                </div>
                <span class="status-badge disabled" id="push-status">載入中</span>
            </div>
            
            <!-- 訂閱/取消按鈕 -->
            <div style="padding: 16px 20px;" id="push-action-container">
                <button class="btn btn-success" id="subscribe-btn" onclick="subscribePush()" style="display: none;">
                    🔔 啟用推播通知
                </button>
                <button class="btn btn-outline" id="unsubscribe-btn" onclick="unsubscribePush()" style="display: none;">
                    🔕 關閉推播通知
                </button>
            </div>
            
            <!-- 不支援提示 -->
            <div class="push-info" id="push-unsupported" style="display: none;">
                ⚠️ 您的瀏覽器不支援推播通知，請使用 Chrome、Firefox、Edge 或 Safari。
            </div>
            
            <!-- 通知開關 -->
            <div id="notify-toggles" style="display: none;">
                <div class="toggle-item">
                    <div class="toggle-label">
                        <span class="toggle-icon">🎰</span>
                        <span class="toggle-text">開獎提醒</span>
                    </div>

                        <input type="checkbox" id="notify-draw" checked onchange="updateNotifySettings()">
                        <span class="toggle-slider"></span>
                    </label>
                </div>
                <div class="toggle-item">
                    <div class="toggle-label">
                        <span class="toggle-icon">🎉</span>
                        <span class="toggle-text">中獎通知</span>
                    </div>
                    <label class="toggle-switch">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="notify-win" checked onchange="updateNotifySettings()">
                        <span class="toggle-slider"></span>
                    </label>
                </div>
                <div class="toggle-item">
                    <div class="toggle-label">
                        <span class="toggle-icon">💰</span>
                        <span class="toggle-text">結算通知</span>
                    </div>
                    <label class="toggle-switch">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="notify-settlement" checked onchange="updateNotifySettings()">
                        <span class="toggle-slider"></span>
                    </label>
                </div>
                
                <!-- 測試通知按鈕 -->
                <div style="padding: 16px 20px;">
                    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="sendTestNotify()">
                        🔔 發送測試通知
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- 其他 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-header">
                <span>ℹ️</span>
                <span>其他</span>
            </div>
            <a href="/personal" class="menu-item">
                <span class="menu-icon">🎫</span>
                <div class="menu-content">
                    <div class="menu-title">個人彩券</div>
                    <div class="menu-desc">管理個人購買的彩券</div>
                </div>
                <span class="menu-arrow">›</span>
            </a>
            <a href="/statistics" class="menu-item">
                <span class="menu-icon">📊</span>
                <div class="menu-content">
                    <div class="menu-title">統計報表</div>
                    <div class="menu-desc">查看投資報酬率</div>
                </div>
                <span class="menu-arrow">›</span>
            </a>
            <div class="menu-item" onclick="logout()">
                <span class="menu-icon" style="background: #FFEBEE;">🚪</span>
                <div class="menu-content">
                    <div class="menu-title" style="color: var(--danger);">登出</div>
                    <div class="menu-desc">登出此帳號</div>
                </div>
                <span class="menu-arrow">›</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </main>
    
    <div id="toast" class="toast"></div>
    
    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        let userData = null;
        let notifySettings = null;
        let swRegistration = null;
        let pushSubscription = null;
        
        // ========== Auth & API ==========
        
        async function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = '/';
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        
        async function apiGet(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            if (!res.ok) {
                if (res.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = '/';
                    return null;
                }
                throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'API Error');
            }
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiPut(url, data) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            if (!res.ok) {
                throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'API Error');
            }
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiPost(url, data = {}) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            if (!res.ok) {
                throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'API Error');
            }
            return res.json();
        }
        
        async function apiDelete(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                method: 'DELETE',
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
            });
            if (!res.ok) {
                throw new Error((await res.json()).detail || 'API Error');
            }
            return res.json();
        }
        
        // ========== Profile ==========
        
        async function loadProfile() {
            try {
                userData = await apiGet('/users/me');
                if (!userData) return;
                
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('profile-avatar');
                if (userData.picture_url) {
                    avatarEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${userData.picture_url}" alt="avatar">`;
                } else {
                    avatarEl.innerHTML = `<span>${(userData.nickname || userData.display_name).charAt(0)}</span>`;
                }
                
                document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = 
                    userData.nickname || userData.display_name;
                
                const roleEl = document.getElementById('profile-role');
                if (userData.role === 'admin') {
                    roleEl.textContent = '👑 系統管理員';
                    roleEl.classList.add('admin');
                } else {
                    roleEl.textContent = '一般會員';
                }
                
                document.getElementById('display-name').value = userData.display_name;
                document.getElementById('nickname').value = userData.nickname || '';
                document.getElementById('email').value = userData.email || '';
                document.getElementById('phone').value = userData.phone || '';
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load profile:', e);
                showToast('載入失敗', 'error');
            }
        }
        
        async function saveProfile() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('save-btn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '儲存中...';
            
            try {
                const data = {
                    nickname: document.getElementById('nickname').value.trim() || null,
                    email: document.getElementById('email').value.trim() || null,
                    phone: document.getElementById('phone').value.trim() || null
                };
                
                userData = await apiPut('/users/me', data);
                
                document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = 
                    userData.nickname || userData.display_name;
                
                showToast('設定已儲存', 'success');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.innerHTML = '💾 儲存變更';
            }
        }
        
        // ========== Web Push ==========
        
        function urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64String) {
            const padding = '='.repeat((4 - base64String.length % 4) % 4);
            const base64 = (base64String + padding)
                .replace(/-/g, '+')
                .replace(/_/g, '/');
            const rawData = window.atob(base64);
            const outputArray = new Uint8Array(rawData.length);
            for (let i = 0; i < rawData.length; ++i) {
                outputArray[i] = rawData.charCodeAt(i);
            }
            return outputArray;
        }
        
        function arrayBufferToBase64(buffer) {
            let binary = '';
            const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
            for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) {
                binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
            }
            return window.btoa(binary);
        }
        
        async function initPushNotifications() {
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('push-status');
            const descEl = document.getElementById('push-desc');
            const unsupportedEl = document.getElementById('push-unsupported');
            const subscribeBtnEl = document.getElementById('subscribe-btn');
            const unsubscribeBtnEl = document.getElementById('unsubscribe-btn');
            const togglesEl = document.getElementById('notify-toggles');
            
            // 檢查瀏覽器支援
            if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator) || !('PushManager' in window)) {
                statusEl.textContent = '不支援';
                statusEl.className = 'status-badge unsupported';
                descEl.textContent = '您的瀏覽器不支援推播通知';
                unsupportedEl.style.display = 'block';
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                // 取得通知設定
                notifySettings = await apiGet('/notify/settings');
                
                if (!notifySettings.push_enabled) {
                    statusEl.textContent = '未啟用';
                    statusEl.className = 'status-badge disabled';
                    descEl.textContent = '系統尚未啟用推播功能';
                    return;
                }
                
                // 註冊 Service Worker
                swRegistration = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/static/sw.js');
                console.log('Service Worker registered');
                
                // 檢查現有訂閱
                pushSubscription = await swRegistration.pushManager.getSubscription();
                
                if (pushSubscription && notifySettings.subscribed) {
                    // 已訂閱
                    statusEl.textContent = '已啟用';
                    statusEl.className = 'status-badge enabled';
                    descEl.textContent = `已在 ${notifySettings.subscription_count} 個裝置上啟用`;
                    unsubscribeBtnEl.style.display = 'block';
                    togglesEl.style.display = 'block';
                    
                    // 設定開關狀態
                    document.getElementById('notify-draw').checked = notifySettings.notify_draw_reminder;
                    document.getElementById('notify-win').checked = notifySettings.notify_win_alert;
                    document.getElementById('notify-settlement').checked = notifySettings.notify_settlement;
                } else {
                    // 未訂閱
                    statusEl.textContent = '未啟用';
                    statusEl.className = 'status-badge disabled';
                    descEl.textContent = '點擊下方按鈕啟用推播通知';
                    subscribeBtnEl.style.display = 'block';
                }
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Push init error:', e);
                statusEl.textContent = '錯誤';
                statusEl.className = 'status-badge unsupported';
                descEl.textContent = '初始化推播功能時發生錯誤';
            }
        }
        
        async function subscribePush() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('subscribe-btn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.textContent = '啟用中...';
            
            try {
                // 請求通知權限
                const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
                if (permission !== 'granted') {
                    showToast('請允許通知權限', 'warning');
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.textContent = '🔔 啟用推播通知';
                    return;
                }
                
                // 訂閱推播
                const subscription = await swRegistration.pushManager.subscribe({
                    userVisibleOnly: true,
                    applicationServerKey: urlBase64ToUint8Array(notifySettings.vapid_public_key)
                });
                
                // 發送到伺服器
                const p256dh = arrayBufferToBase64(subscription.getKey('p256dh'));
                const auth = arrayBufferToBase64(subscription.getKey('auth'));
                
                await apiPost('/notify/subscribe', {
                    endpoint: subscription.endpoint,
                    p256dh: p256dh,
                    auth: auth,
                    device_name: getDeviceName()
                });
                
                pushSubscription = subscription;
                showToast('推播通知已啟用！', 'success');
                
                // 重新載入狀態
                await initPushNotifications();
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Subscribe error:', e);
                showToast('啟用失敗：' + e.message, 'error');
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = '🔔 啟用推播通知';
            }
        }
        
        async function unsubscribePush() {
            if (!confirm('確定要關閉推播通知嗎？')) return;
            
            const btn = document.getElementById('unsubscribe-btn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.textContent = '處理中...';
            
            try {
                if (pushSubscription) {
                    await apiDelete(`/notify/subscribe?endpoint=${encodeURIComponent(pushSubscription.endpoint)}`);
                    await pushSubscription.unsubscribe();
                    pushSubscription = null;
                }
                
                showToast('已關閉推播通知', 'success');
                await initPushNotifications();
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Unsubscribe error:', e);
                showToast('關閉失敗', 'error');
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = '🔕 關閉推播通知';
            }
        }
        
        function getDeviceName() {
            const ua = navigator.userAgent;
            if (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(ua)) return 'iPhone/iPad';
            if (/Android/.test(ua)) return 'Android';
            if (/Windows/.test(ua)) return 'Windows';
            if (/Mac/.test(ua)) return 'Mac';
            return '未知裝置';
        }
        
        async function updateNotifySettings() {
            try {
                await apiPut('/notify/settings', {
                    notify_draw_reminder: document.getElementById('notify-draw').checked,
                    notify_win_alert: document.getElementById('notify-win').checked,
                    notify_settlement: document.getElementById('notify-settlement').checked
                });
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to update notify settings:', e);
            }
        }
        
        async function sendTestNotify() {
            try {
                const result = await apiPost('/notify/test');
                showToast(result.message, 'success');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast(e.message, 'error');
            }
        }
        
        // ========== Utils ==========
        
        function logout() {
            if (confirm('確定要登出嗎？')) {
                localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
                window.location.href = '/';
            }
        }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.textContent = message;
            toast.className = `toast ${type} show`;
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }
        
        // ========== Init ==========
        
        async function init() {
            if (!await checkAuth()) return;
            await loadProfile();
            await initPushNotifications();
        }
        
        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

        .legend-dot {

        /* Empty State */

<body>
    <header class="header">
        <a href="/dashboard" class="back-btn">←</a>

    <main>
        <!-- ROI Card -->

    <script>
        const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
        
        // Auth Check
        async function checkAuth() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = '/';
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        
        // API Helper
        async function apiGet(url) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${url}`, {
                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                if (!res.ok) {
                    if (res.status === 401) {
                        window.location.href = '/';
                        return null;
                    }
                    throw new Error('API Error');
                }
                return res.json();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('API Error:', e);
                return null;
            }
        }
        
        // Tab Switching
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(tab => {
            tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
                // Update tabs
                document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
                tab.classList.add('active');
                
                // Update content
                const tabId = tab.dataset.tab;
                document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
            });
        });
        
        // Load Overall Stats
        async function loadOverallStats() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/statistics/overall');
                if (!data) return;
                
                // ROI
                const roiEl = document.getElementById('roi-value');
                roiEl.textContent = `${data.roi_percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${data.roi_percent}%`;
                roiEl.className = `roi-value ${data.roi_percent >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}`;
                
                const profitText = data.total_profit >= 0 
                    ? `獲利 $${Number(data.total_profit).toLocaleString()}`
                    : `虧損 $${Math.abs(Number(data.total_profit)).toLocaleString()}`;
                document.getElementById('roi-detail').textContent = profitText;
                
                // Stats
                document.getElementById('total-invested').textContent = `$${Number(data.total_invested).toLocaleString()}`;
                document.getElementById('total-prize').textContent = `$${Number(data.total_prize).toLocaleString()}`;
                document.getElementById('win-rate').textContent = `${data.win_rate}%`;
                document.getElementById('periods-count').textContent = data.periods_participated;
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load overall stats:', e);
            }
        }
        
        // Load Monthly Stats
        async function loadMonthlyStats() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/statistics/monthly?months=6');
                if (!data) return;
                
                const container = document.getElementById('chart-bars');
                
                if (data.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">📈</div>
                            <p class="empty-text">尚無數據</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                // 找最大值
                let maxVal = 0;
                data.forEach(d => {
                    maxVal = Math.max(maxVal, Math.abs(Number(d.invested)), Math.abs(Number(d.prize)));
                });
                maxVal = maxVal || 1;
                
                // 反轉順序（最新在右）
                const reversed = [...data].reverse();
                
                container.innerHTML = reversed.map(d => {
                    const investedHeight = Math.abs(Number(d.invested)) / maxVal * 100;
                    const prizeHeight = Math.abs(Number(d.prize)) / maxVal * 100;
                    const month = d.year_month.split('-')[1];
                    
                    return `
                        <div class="chart-bar-group">
                            <div style="display: flex; gap: 2px; align-items: flex-end; height: 100px;">
                                <div class="chart-bar invested" style="height: ${Math.max(investedHeight, 2)}%;"></div>
                                <div class="chart-bar prize" style="height: ${Math.max(prizeHeight, 2)}%;"></div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="chart-label">${parseInt(month)}月</div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load monthly stats:', e);
            }
        }
        
        // Load Series Performance
        async function loadSeriesPerformance() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/statistics/series-performance');
                if (!data) return;
                
                const container = document.getElementById('perf-list');
                
                if (data.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">🎰</div>
                            <p class="empty-text">尚未參與任何系列團</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                container.innerHTML = data.map(s => `
                    <div class="perf-item">
                        <div class="perf-info">
                            <div class="perf-name">${s.series_name}</div>
                            <div class="perf-detail">
                                ${s.periods_count} 期 · 
                                投資 $${Number(s.invested).toLocaleString()} · 
                                份額 $${Number(s.current_pool_share).toLocaleString()}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="perf-roi">
                            <div class="perf-roi-value ${s.roi_percent >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                                ${s.roi_percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${s.roi_percent}%
                            </div>
                            <div class="perf-profit">
                                ${s.profit >= 0 ? '+' : ''}$${Number(s.profit).toLocaleString()}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load series performance:', e);
            }
        }
        
        // Load Winning Records
        async function loadWinningRecords() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/statistics/winning-records?limit=20');
                if (!data) return;
                
                const container = document.getElementById('win-list');
                
                if (data.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">🎉</div>
                            <p class="empty-text">尚無中獎記錄</p>
                            <p class="empty-text" style="margin-top: 8px; font-size: 12px;">繼續加油，好運即將到來！🍀</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                container.innerHTML = data.map(w => `
                    <div class="win-item">
                        <div class="win-header">
                            <div class="win-title">${w.series_name} 第${w.period_number}期</div>
                            <div class="win-amount">+$${Number(w.my_share).toLocaleString()}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="win-detail">
                            ${w.lottery_type}
                            ${w.draw_date ? ` · ${w.draw_date}` : ''}
                            · 總獎金 $${Number(w.prize_amount).toLocaleString()}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load winning records:', e);
            }
        }
        
        // Load Lottery Type Stats
        async function loadLotteryTypeStats() {
            try {
                const data = await apiGet('/statistics/by-lottery-type');
                if (!data) return;
                
                const container = document.getElementById('lottery-list');
                
                if (data.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="empty-state">
                            <div class="empty-icon">🎫</div>
                            <p class="empty-text">尚無數據</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                
                container.innerHTML = data.map(l => `
                    <div class="perf-item">
                        <div class="perf-info">
                            <div class="perf-name">${l.lottery_name}</div>
                            <div class="perf-detail">
                                ${l.periods} 期 · 
                                投資 $${Number(l.invested).toLocaleString()} · 
                                獎金 $${Number(l.prize).toLocaleString()}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="perf-roi">
                            <div class="perf-roi-value ${l.roi_percent >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                                ${l.roi_percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${l.roi_percent}%
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
                
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to load lottery type stats:', e);
            }
        }
        
        // Initialize
        async function init() {
            if (!await checkAuth()) return;
            
            // Load all data
            loadOverallStats();
            loadMonthlyStats();
            loadSeriesPerformance();
            loadWinningRecords();
            loadLotteryTypeStats();
        }
        
        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <header class="header">
        <div class="header-left">
